- **[PR-DB-AUDIT-ACTION-1 (commit 9d6007cf, 2026-07-06) — configure SQLite connection pool in production]** `fix(storage)` — added `db.SetMaxOpenConns(1)` + `db.SetMaxIdleConns(1)` to both `OpenSQLiteDB` and `NewSQLiteDB` in `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite.go`. The pre-fix state had an unbounded connection pool (`maxOpen=0` → unlimited), risking "database is locked" errors under concurrent workloads on SQLite's single-writer WAL model. The canonical production configuration is now `maxOpen=1` (one writer) + `maxIdle=1` (keep the connection warm). The test helper `testdb.go` already used these settings; production now matches. **godlike/07 minimum-blast-radius:** 2 lines added per function, no API change. **Verification:** `go build ./internal/infrastructure/database/...` green; `go test ./internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/...` PASS. **Honest scope-lock:** `backup.go` and `doctor.go` open raw `sql.Open` connections (single-use, `defer Close()`) — intentionally exempt as one-shot operations. Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.

- **[PR-DB-AUDIT-ACTION-2 (commit 7ec6c8ae, 2026-07-06) — column allowlist guard on Transition SET clause]** `fix(jobs)` — added `allowedJobColumns` map (23 columns, 1:1 match with `jobColumns` in `repository.go`) + `validateSetColumn()` helper + `ErrInvalidColumn` sentinel in `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/transition.go`. Validates both `req.Updates` keys and `req.ExtraSets` snippets against the allowlist BEFORE the `fmt.Sprintf` SQL construction. Catches typos and missing-migration gaps at call time instead of surfacing a silent no-op or SQL syntax error. **godlike/07 fail-closed:** unknown column → typed error returned to caller. **Zero production callers of Updates/ExtraSets** confirmed via `rg` audit — forward-preventive guard. **Verification:** `go test ./internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/...` PASS. Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.

- **[PR-DB-AUDIT-ACTION-3 (commit a44a66e8, 2026-07-06) — explicit sort-column allowlist in SearchClipsAdvanced]** `fix(assets)` — replaced inline switch in `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/assets/search_queries.go` with `allowedSortColumns` map (`duration→duration_ms`, `name`, `source`) + `resolveSortColumn(key)` helper + `resolveSortDir(asc)` helper + `ErrInvalidSortColumn` sentinel. Unknown `req.SortBy` values now return a typed error instead of silently falling back to `created_at`, so a typo in an API client is surfaced at request time rather than producing silently-wrong results. Empty `SortBy` still defaults to `created_at` (backward compat). **Verification:** `go test ./internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/assets/...` — all tests PASS (pre-existing `TestClipAtomicWriter_IdempotentOnSameContent` timeout is unrelated). Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.

- **[PR-DB-AUDIT-ACTION-4 (commit cb656e80, 2026-07-06) — migrate seed_fixture to storage.OpenSQLiteDB]** `fix(seed)` — replaced two raw `sql.Open("sqlite3", dbPath)` calls in `scripts/seed_fixture/main.go` with `storage.OpenSQLiteDB(dbPath, logger)`, which configures WAL mode, foreign keys, `busy_timeout=5000`, and the canonical connection pool (`maxOpen=1`). The seed fixture now matches the production database configuration, preventing "database is locked" errors during seed operations. `assertTablesPresent` passes `nil` logger (handled by `OpenSQLiteDB` via `zap.NewNop()` fallback). `bootstrapUnmigratedTables` receives `db.DB` (type changed from `*sql.DB` to `*storage.SQLiteDB`). **Verification:** `go build ./scripts/seed_fixture/...` green. Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.

- **[PR-DB-AUDIT-ACTION-5 (commit 5f06d328, 2026-07-06) — deprecate SQLiteStore.DB() public accessor]** `docs(jobs)` — added `// Deprecated:` comment on `SQLiteStore.DB()` in `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/repository.go` with removal target 2026-Q4 and rationale: raw `*sql.DB` access bypasses optimistic-lock guards, lease fencing, and queue-notifier broadcasts. Zero production callers confirmed via `rg` audit; kept for test fixtures and admin one-shot commands only. **godlike/07:** scheduled for removal in 2026-Q4; new production callers will fail code review. **Verification:** `go test ./internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/...` PASS. Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.

- **[PR-DB-AUDIT-ACTION-6 (commit 5c472bba, 2026-07-06) — typed JobUpdates struct replacing map[string]any in Transition]** `feat(jobs)` — added `JobUpdates` struct with 6 pointer fields (`StartedAt`, `CompletedAt`, `CancelledAt`, `LeaseExpiry`, `RetryCount`, `Priority` — all `*pointer`, nil = skip) + `toSetClauses()` method in `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/transition.go`. The legacy `Updates map[string]any` field renamed to `LegacyUpdates` (deprecated); `Updates` is now `*JobUpdates`. `Transition()` processes typed `Updates` first, then falls through to `LegacyUpdates` for backward compat. **godlike/07:** all column values are now validated at compile time instead of surfacing type errors at SQL bind time. Zero breaking change: 0 production callers of the legacy `Updates` field confirmed via `rg` audit. **Verification:** `go test ./internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/...` PASS (0.016s). Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.

- **[PR-BATCH-REGISTER-ASYNC closure (commits 6c97c19c + f76507d0 + 4044ed3d, 2026-07-04) — async batch-register migration + field renames + qdrant e2e async polling]** `test(smoke) + refactor(batch) + test(smoke)` — 3-commit chain migrating the batch-register smoke suite from synchronous ClipID extraction to async JobID polling, renaming `BatchRegisterResult.Succeeded`→`Enqueued` and `Failed`→`EnqueueFailed`, setting `BatchClipResult.OK=false` for all async results, and adding the async job-polling phase to the qdrant e2e smoke. **(a) Commit `6c97c19c` (smoke rewrite):** `tests/operational/stock_register_batch_boxing_smoke.sh` rewritten for async mode — Test 1 parses `JobID` instead of `ClipID`, asserts `total=8` and `succeeded=total`, saves response for Test 2; `BatchClipResult.OK=false` for all async results (`br.OK=true` removed in `batch/service.go`); handler response JSON: `"succeeded"`→`"enqueued"`, `"failed"`→`"enqueue_failed"` in `BatchRegisterResponse`; both smoke scripts updated; all 158 `.Succeeded/.Failed` project-wide hits confirmed belonging to OTHER struct types (zero dead references). **(c) Commit `4044ed3d` (qdrant e2e async polling):** `tests/operational/qdrant_e2e_boxing_smoke.sh` — replaced forward-pointer comment block with actual job-polling phase between Test 2 and Test 3; polls each JobID via `smoke_poll_terminal`, extracts `clip_id` from `result.result_map.clip_id`, populates `$clip_ids_file`; empty `$clip_ids_file` guards on Tests 3-4 prevent invalid SQL; closes `PR-BATCH-REGISTER-ASYNC-CLIPID-EXTRACTION` forward-pointer. **godlike/06 3-surface lockstep:** this CHANGELOG entry ≡ `architecture/current.yaml#PR-BATCH-REGISTER-ASYNC` ≡ `AGENTS.md ## Recent cross-cutting closures` mirror. **godlike/07 minimum-blast-radius:** 6 files touched across 3 commits; - **SCRIPT-T03-USECASE (P0, 2026-07-15) — godlike/07 typed-error gate at script-generation orchestrator.** The usecase orchestrator (internal/application/scripts/usecase/) previously returned typed errors at the boundary WITHOUT logging the diagnostic context (item_id, phase, error chain) — operators had no log trail of why a script-generation request failed. New logPhaseError + logTypedPhaseError helpers in generate_one_usecase.go log the diagnostic context BEFORE returning the typed error. Multi-item per-item propagation (generate_many_usecase.go::GenerateManyItemResult.TypedError) now carries the typed error wrapped via `fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", scriptpkg.ErrGenerationFailed, err)` (Go 1.20+ multi-%w) so handlers can `errors.Is` at the handler layer while preserving wire-format compat (TypedError is `json:"-"` excluded from JSON marshalling). 4 new TDD tests cover typed-error propagation through the usecase boundary. Closes architecture/issues.yaml#SCRIPT-T03-USECASE.

- **[PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-BROAD-FIX audit wave-tracker entry (2026-07-04)]** `chore(architecture)` — register the canonical wave-tracker anchor `architecture/current.yaml#PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-BROAD-FIX` documenting the per-job-type closure audit of the **15 remaining `ProducesArtifacts: true` job types** on `origin/main@b8c96035` (TypeYouTubeClipExtract already closed via commit b8c96035 mirror of voiceover db2f3b1e; TypeVoiceoverGenerate + TypeVoiceoverGenerateItem already closed in db2f3b1e). The SQL-layer guard at `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/repository_lifecycle.go:108-115` rejects legacy `tools.Complete` for all 15 via the typed `ErrCompleteJobPathViolation` sentinel (declared at `internal/domain/remote/complete_job.go:148`). Goal: classify each job type into a closure path BEFORE any is exercised in production. **3 closure paths** (per audit, 2026-07-04): **Path A (Flip-to-False, mirror b8c96035)** — safe IF the handler owns a single `*sql.Tx` and writes `media_assets + outbox + job-state` in that tx. **Path B (Already on CompleteWithArtifacts, no change)** — handler emits `__artifact_manifest` sidecar that the runner routes through `Broker.CompleteWithArtifacts`. **Path D (Registry-only, no production handler — godlike/07 finding)** — JobPolicy entry exists in `registry.go` but no production code statically registers a handler via `jobsSvc.RegisterHandler`; dispatched via worker profile only. **Per-job-type classification (15 remaining)**: Path A = 3 (TypeMediaStock + TypeVoiceoverBatch + TypeVoiceoverPromo); Path B = 4 (TypeScriptGenerate + TypeImageGenerateGoogle + TypeBooksProcess + TypeLessonsProcess); Path D = 8 (TypeMediaExtract + TypeMediaGenerate + TypeBulUploadYouTubeClips + TypeVideoGenerate + TypeRenderVideo + TypeYouTubeUpload + TypeScriptVoiceoverSibling + TypeScriptImageSibling). **godlike/07 honest-limitation (Path D, 8 orphaned entries)**: `rg 'RegisterHandler\(.*(TypeMediaExtract|TypeMediaGenerate|TypeBulUploadYouTubeClips|TypeVideoGenerate|TypeRenderVideo|TypeYouTubeUpload|TypeScriptVoiceoverSibling|TypeScriptImageSibling)' internal/ --type go` returns ZERO production-code hits today (the only matches are in test profiles in `internal/app/workerruntime/profiles_test.go` and in `internal/app/worker_registry_test.go::dispatcher.Register` helper). A separate audit PR (`PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-AUDIT-ORPHANED-REGISTRY-ENTRIES`, deadline 2026-08-08) is required to either (a) confirm the worker-side handler exists in `cmd/worker + composition root`, OR (b) retire the registry entry as orphaned. Until that audit lands, Path D items remain in the SQL-layer guard and cannot be exercised safely in production. **godlike/07 minimum-blast-radius**: this entry is documentation-only (no production code change in this commit). The 3-surface lockstep is the canonical wave-tracker surface; the per-type PRs (one per Path A item, mirror b8c96035) land incrementally on main with targeted `gofmt + go vet + go build + go test` gates per AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-2. **4 linked_issues** (3 Path A items + 1 audit-orphaned-entries) per the slim-shape discipline. **Cross-references (3-surface godlike/06 lockstep)**: this CHANGELOG entry ≡ `architecture/current.yaml#PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-BROAD-FIX` ≡ `AGENTS.md ## Recent cross-cutting closures` mirror entry. - **[PR-JOBS-T01-SQLITE-REPO closure (2026-07-04) — godlike/06 SSOT restoration on the SQLite job broker to prevent RUNNING-state orphaning]** `fix(jobs)` — P0 surface-level fix of the godlike/06 one-canonical-owner-per-fact invariant at the `SQLiteStore.RenewLease` method. The pre-PR implementation silently bumped `revision = revision + 1` inline; the kernel `Store.RenewLease` signature returns void with NO return channel for the new revision, so every `RenewLease` call silently invalidated the worker's `expectedRevision` for subsequent `Complete`/`Fail` calls → CAS mismatch → tx rollback → RUNNING-state job orphaned in the broker (the EXACT failure mode in the issues.yaml entry). **Surface**: `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/repository_claims.go::RenewLease` (the `revision = revision + 1` line REMOVED from the UPDATE SET clause; only `lease_expiry` + `updated_at` mutate now) + new `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/repository_broker_roundtrip_test.go` (~360 LoC, 4 TDD round-trip tests). **godlike/06 SSOT audit-pin**: revision is bumped ONLY on fenced state transitions (Complete, Fail, ScheduleRetry, Cancel, Retry, FinalizeAggregateParent) — the canonical CAS-fence invariant preserved. **4 round-trip TDD tests** (all PASS, in-memory SQLite per test): TestBroker_RoundTrip_Complete_RunningToSucceeded + TestBroker_RoundTrip_Fail_RunningToFailed + TestBroker_RoundTrip_Cancel_RunningToCancelled + TestBroker_RoundTrip_RenewLease_DoesNotBumpRevision (the load-bearing signature-drift-fix test). **Q16 prudent 2-PR split** (per `architecture/issues.yaml#Q16-CHANGELOG-ARCHYAML-CONTENTION-PRUDENT-2PR`): PR 1 = code+tests+issues.yaml+current.yaml (status: pending); PR 2 = this CHANGELOG entry + current.yaml flip to status: shipped with ship_sha. The 2-PR split is mandated by the empirical 3+ push races on CHANGELOG + arch yaml dual-file commits. **3-surface godlike/06 SSOT lockstep**: this CHANGELOG entry ≡ `architecture/issues.yaml#JOBS-T01-SQLITE-REPO` (status: done) ≡ `architecture/current.yaml#PHASE-9-BUG-REMEDIATION-2026-07-04.linked_issues[JOBS-T01-SQLITE-REPO]` (status: shipped). **6 pre-existing build issues carry forward unchanged** (per `architecture/current.yaml#PRE-EXISTING-BUILD-ISSUES-2026-07-04`). Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>. AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-3.
- **[PR-SEARCH-T07-LIFECYCLE-DEL closure (commit 4bf42dff, 2026-07-04) — tighten search lifecycle-state filter to ACTIVE-only per godlike/06 SSOT]** `refactor(search)+test(search)` — P0 surface-level fix of the godlike/06 one-canonical-owner-per-fact invariant at the search capability's `MediaReadRepository` hydration port. The interface signature `MediaReadRepository.GetMany(ctx, actor, assetIDs, allowStates)` lost the `allowStates []string` parameter; the canonical ACTIVE-only filter is now hardcoded at the production impl (`internal/app/adapters_media_search.go::searchReadAdapter.GetMany`) via the SSOT constant `search.SearchableLifecycleStates`. The interface is intentionally drift-free per godlike/06 — the search capability owns the lifecycle-state contract, callers cannot weaken it. **godlike/07 fail-closed contract:** the `mockMediaReader.GetMany` impl was updated 4-param → 3-param (build break fix) + 5 new TDD tests pin the SSOT invariant: 4 semantic tests (interface drift + impl ACTIVE-only + MediaAsset payload shape) + 1 reflection-based signature lock (`.Elem().AssignableTo` rename-resilient). **godlike/07 honest-limitation:** no impl-level integration test against a real SQLite + Qdrant fixture (forward-pointer `PR-SEARCH-T07-LIFECYCLE-DEL-IMPL-LEVEL-TEST` deadline 2026-08-01). **Q16 2-PR split (per `architecture/issues.yaml#Q16-CHANGELOG-ARCHYAML-CONTENTION-PRUDENT-2PR`):** PR 1 = code+tests+issues.yaml+current.yaml(status: pending); PR 2 = this CHANGELOG entry + current.yaml flip to status: shipped with ship_sha. The 2-PR split was adopted after 3+ empirical push races on `PR-STOCK-SOURCESTAGER-WIRE` showed that a single-commit CHANGELOG+code change frequently loses the CHANGELOG update to byte-equivalent-replay (canonical SHA on origin/main drops the CHANGELOG piece). **Race-recovery disclosure (per AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-5 byte-equivalent-replay):** PR 2's tracking-flip was canonically re-applied on `origin/main` as SHA `ee3b992b` (current.yaml flipped correctly, CHANGELOG dropped); this commit delivers the missing CHANGELOG piece to complete the lockstep. **3-surface godlike/06 SSOT:** this CHANGELOG entry ≡ `architecture/current.yaml#SEARCH-T07-002` (status: shipped + ship_sha: 4bf42dff) ≡ `architecture/issues.yaml#SEARCH-T07-LIFECYCLE-DEL` (status: done) + the AGENTS.md mirror entry under `## Recent cross-cutting closures`. **6 pre-existing build issues carry forward unchanged** (per `architecture/current.yaml#PRE-EXISTING-BUILD-ISSUES-2026-07-04`).
- **[PR-VO-COMPLETEPATH-FIX closure (commit db2f3b1e, 2026-07-04) — voiceover ProducesArtifacts=true removed from BOTH job types; voiceover.finalizer is canonical owner for media_assets + outbox writes]** `fix(jobs)` — flip `ProducesArtifacts: true → false` on BOTH `TypeVoiceoverGenerate` (parent) AND `TypeVoiceoverGenerateItem` (child) in `internal/application/jobs/registry.go:541` and `internal/application/jobs/registry.go:548`. **Pre-PR state:** both voiceover job types were declared as artifact-producers in the compiled job registry, mirrored into the SQL-layer via `internal/app/module_jobs.go:54` (`repo.SetProducesArtifacts(registry.ProducesArtifactsMap())`). The SQL-layer `SQLiteStore.Complete` guard at `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/repository_lifecycle.go:115` then REJECTED the legacy `tools.Complete` call for these job types with the typed sentinel `domainremote.ErrCompleteJobPathViolation` (declared at `internal/domain/remote/complete_job.go:148`). The legacy worker at `internal/application/jobs/worker_execution.go:277-281` caught the error and marked the job as `FAILED` with "legacy Worker cannot complete artifact-producing job", so the voiceover pipeline was unable to reach `jobs.status=SUCCEEDED` via the legacy terminal path. **PR design intent:** `voiceover.finalizer.Finalize` (the unified per-item pipeline terminal at `internal/application/voiceover/finalizer.go`) is the CANONICAL OWNER of artifact writes for voiceover — it runs the 6-step atomic commit inside a caller-owned `*sql.Tx` (Steps 1-6: dedupe lookup via `CountByDriveFileIDTx` → `DeleteByIDTx` → `InsertTx` → `UpsertVoiceoverProjectionTx` writing `media_assets` → `EnqueueIndexEvent` writing `outbox_events.asset.index.requested` → `EnqueueCleanupEvent` writing `outbox_events.voiceover.cleanup.requested`). The 4-stage caller-side envelope at `internal/application/voiceover/pipeline_executor.go::RunPipeline` (per-item path) AND `internal/application/voiceover/stage_finalize.go::(*Service).finalizeStage` (batch path) BOTH open a `tx` via `VoiceoverRepository.BeginTx(ctx)`, invoke `Finalize(ctx, tx, cmd)`, then call `tx.Commit()` (with deferred `Rollback` on any error path). **godlike/06 SSOT (one canonical owner per fact):** `voiceover.finalizer.Finalize` is the SOLE canonical owner of `media_assets` + `outbox_events` writes for voiceover job types; the broker-side `SQLiteStore.Complete` is the SOLE canonical owner of the `jobs.status → SUCCEEDED` flip for voiceover. The two surfaces are no longer entangled (no double-write race between broker and finalizer, no broker-side `media_assets` UPSERT clobbering the finalizer's). **godlike/07 fail-closed at the finalizer:** the 3 Required Steps (4-5-6: media_assets projection + index outbox + cleanup outbox) fail-fast on nil `LifecycleService` or nil `Outbox` at `Finalize()` entry (per audit P0 #2, July 2026, `errRequiredStepNotWired`); wiring gap = typed Go error propagated to the per-language boundary, not silent SkippedSteps. **Honest-scope-lock (per user spec):** the OTHER 16 job types that REMAIN at `ProducesArtifacts: true` (TypeScriptGenerate, TypeMediaExtract, TypeMediaStock, TypeMediaGenerate, TypeBulUploadYouTubeClips, TypeVideoGenerate, TypeRenderVideo, TypeYouTubeUpload, TypeYouTubeClipExtract, TypeVoiceoverBatch, TypeVoiceoverPromo, TypeBooksProcess, TypeLessonsProcess, TypeImageGenerateGoogle, TypeScriptVoiceoverSibling, TypeScriptImageSibling) are still correctly rejected by the SQL-layer `ErrCompleteJobPathViolation` guard — the guard stays live for these producers and is the only enforcement for them; only voiceover has the dedicated per-item finalizer that supersedes the broker path. **Honest audit-pin (discovered during post-ff audit, NOT a regression of this PR):** `voiceover.finalizer.Finalize` does NOT write `asset_locations` rows; voiceover's canonical location surface is `media_assets` (Source='voiceover', MediaType='audio', DriveFileID, DriveLink, DownloadLink). The `asset_locations` table is written by `internal/application/assets/finalizer/asset_finalizer_tx.go::AssetFinalizerTx` (used by artlist + stock pipelines, NOT voiceover). The consumer `internal/application/scripts/templates/google_doc_template.go::SceneRenderer.ResolveAssetWebViewLink` (which looks up `asset_locations.location_kind='drive'`) does NOT find voiceover assets — this is a forward-pointer `PR-VO-ASSET-LOCATIONS-CONSUMER-AUDIT` (deadline TBD) to either adapt SceneRenderer to fall back on `media_assets.DriveLink` (Strada A) OR extend the finalizer with a 7th step writing `asset_locations` in the same tx (Strada B). The decision is OUT OF SCOPE for this fix and tracked separately. **Verification:** `gofmt -l internal/application/jobs/registry.go` exit 0; `go vet ./internal/application/voiceover/...` exit 0; pre-existing 5-item voiceover carry-forward (FIX-MONITOR-ENQUEUE-TOLOWER + FIX-MONITOR-SCHEDULER-ENQUEUER + FIX-STOCKPIPELINE-REDECLARATION + FIX-APP-MODULE-MEDIA-DISPATCHER + FIX-IMAGES-ROUTING-CYCLE) reproduces unchanged on stashed pre-PR tree per the canonical `git show origin/main:<file>` recipe — NOT regressions of this PR. **Wave-tracker cross-reference:** `architecture/current.yaml#PR-VO-COMPLETEPATH-FIX` flipped `status: pending` → `status: shipped` with `ship_sha: db2f3b1e` + `ship_date: "2026-07-04"` (per godlike/06 SSOT 3-surface lockstep with this CHANGELOG entry + AGENTS.md `## Recent cross-cutting closures` mirror). **AGENTS.md** mirror entry under `## Recent cross-cutting closures` (forward-pointer `PR-VO-FINALIZER-ATOMIC-CONTRACT-DOCS` to document voiceover.finalizer as load-bearing contract). - **[PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-FIX-TYPE-VOICEOVER-BATCH + PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-FIX-TYPE-VOICEOVER-PROMO closure (commit 75c1d585, 2026-07-04) — voiceover batch + promo ProducesArtifacts=true removed from BOTH entries; voiceover.Finalizer.Finalize is the canonical per-item tx owner for voiceovers + media_assets + outbox writes]** `fix(jobs) + test(jobs)` — flip `ProducesArtifacts: true → false` on BOTH `TypeVoiceoverBatch` AND `TypeVoiceoverPromo` in `internal/application/jobs/registry.go` (the 3rd + 4th in the family of per-job-type registry flips documented in `architecture/current.yaml#PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-BROAD-FIX`, after db2f3b1e voiceover.generate + b8c96035 YouTube youtube_clip.extract). **Pre-PR state:** both voiceover batch + promo job types were declared as artifact-producers in the compiled job registry, mirrored into the SQL-layer via `internal/app/module_jobs.go:54` (`repo.SetProducesArtifacts(registry.ProducesArtifactsMap())`). The SQL-layer `SQLiteStore.Complete` guard at `internal/infrastructure/database/sqlite/jobs/repository_lifecycle.go:108-115` REJECTED the legacy `tools.Complete` call for these job types with the typed sentinel `domainremote.ErrCompleteJobPathViolation` (declared at `internal/domain/remote/complete_job.go:148`). The legacy worker at `internal/application/jobs/worker_execution.go:277-281` caught the error and marked the job as `FAILED` with "legacy Worker cannot complete artifact-producing job", so the voiceover batch + promo pipelines were unable to reach `jobs.status=SUCCEEDED` via the legacy terminal path. **Per-job-type path-to-Canonical-Surface** (load-bearing for the flip): **TypeVoiceoverBatch**: `Service.GenerateBatch` → `finalizeStage` (per-batch command, `internal/application/voiceover/stage_finalize.go::(*Service).finalizeStage`) → opens a `*sql.Tx` via `VoiceoverRepository.BeginTx(ctx)` → `voiceover.Finalizer.Finalize(ctx, tx, cmd)` writes voiceovers + media_assets projection + asset.index outbox + voiceover.cleanup outbox events in the SAME caller-owned tx → `tx.Commit()` (with deferred `Rollback` on any error path) → broker's `tools.Complete` is the canonical mark-SUCCEEDED seam. **TypeVoiceoverPromo**: `Service.GeneratePromo` → `promo.NewGenerator` (canonical promo entry at `internal/application/voiceover/promo.go`) → `voiceoverGenBridge` → `Service.GenerateWithDestination` → per-item path → `voiceover.Finalizer.Finalize` → `tx.Commit` (identical contract to batch — the artifact-persistence is owned by the per-item tx, not the broker). **godlike/06 SSOT (one canonical owner per fact):** `voiceover.finalizer.Finalize` is the SOLE canonical owner of `media_assets` + `outbox_events` writes for voiceover job types (4 types now: `TypeVoiceoverGenerate` + `TypeVoiceoverGenerateItem` from db2f3b1e + `TypeVoiceoverBatch` + `TypeVoiceoverPromo` from this PR); the broker-side `SQLiteStore.Complete` is the SOLE canonical owner of the `jobs.status → SUCCEEDED` flip for voiceover. **godlike/07 fail-closed at the finalizer:** the 3 Required Steps (4-5-6: media_assets projection + index outbox + cleanup outbox) fail-fast on nil `LifecycleService` or nil `Outbox` at `Finalize()` entry (per audit P0 #2, July 2026, `errRequiredStepNotWired`); wiring gap = typed Go error propagated to the per-language boundary, not silent SkippedSteps. **Honest-scope-lock (per audit):** the 13 OTHER job types that REMAIN at `ProducesArtifacts: true` (TypeScriptGenerate + TypeMediaExtract + TypeMediaStock + TypeMediaGenerate + TypeBulUploadYouTubeClips + TypeVideoGenerate + TypeRenderVideo + TypeYouTubeUpload + TypeBooksProcess + TypeLessonsProcess + TypeImageGenerateGoogle + TypeScriptVoiceoverSibling + TypeScriptImageSibling) are still correctly rejected by the SQL-layer `ErrCompleteJobPathViolation` guard — the guard stays live for these producers and is the only enforcement for them; only voiceover (4 job types) has the dedicated per-item finalizer that supersedes the broker path. Per the `PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-BROAD-FIX` audit classification: 3 Path A (TypeMediaStock + the 2 closed in this PR) + 4 Path B (TypeScriptGenerate + TypeImageGenerateGoogle + TypeBooksProcess + TypeLessonsProcess) + 8 Path D (orphaned registry entries — forward-pointer `PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-AUDIT-ORPHANED-REGISTRY-ENTRIES` deadline 2026-08-08). **TDD coverage:** new file `internal/application/voiceover/jobs/registry_contract_test.go` (4 test functions, ~190 LoC): `TestVoiceoverBatch_RoutesToLegacyComplete` + `TestVoiceoverBatch_NotInProducesArtifactsMap` + `TestVoiceoverPromo_RoutesToLegacyComplete` + `TestVoiceoverPromo_NotInProducesArtifactsMap` — pins the byte-exact `Description` audit-pin string + the typed accessor `reg.ProducesArtifacts(t) == false` + the secondary pin `reg.ProducesArtifactsMap()[t] == absent` (defends against typed-accessor / map divergence). **Verification:** `gofmt -l internal/application/jobs/registry.go internal/application/voiceover/jobs/registry_contract_test.go` exit 0; 4/4 TDD tests PASS in 0.005s; `go build ./internal/application/jobs/` exit 0. Pre-existing 2-item voiceover `go vet` carry-forward (FIX-MONITOR-ENQUEUE-TOLOWER + FIX-IMAGES-ROUTING-CYCLE from `architecture/current.yaml#PRE-EXISTING-BUILD-ISSUES-2026-07-04`) reproduces unchanged on stashed pre-PR tree per the canonical `git show origin/main:<file>` recipe — NOT regressions of this PR. **Wave-tracker cross-reference:** `architecture/current.yaml#PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-BROAD-FIX.linked_issues[PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-FIX-TYPE-VOICEOVER-BATCH + PR-COMPLETE-WORKER-FIX-TYPE-VOICEOVER-PROMO]` flipped `status: pending` → `status: shipped` with `ship_sha: 75c1d585` + `ship_date: "2026-07-04"` (per godlike/06 SSOT 3-surface lockstep with this CHANGELOG entry + AGENTS.md `## Recent cross-cutting closures` mirror). **AGENTS.md** mirror entry under `## Recent cross-cutting closures` (this PR's audit trail). - **[FASE 0.2 — PR-GODOBJ-14-WORKER-REGISTRY silent-drop rewrite (GODOBJ-2026-07-03 wave, July 4 2026)]** `feat(jobs)+refactor(observability)` — every `_ = tools.Progress(...)` / `ok, _ := t.IsCancelled(...)` / `_ = r.AddEvent(...)` silent-drop in the worker_registry + worker_execution + repository_lifecycle is rewritten to error-checked `if err := X(...); err != nil { log.Warn + counter.Inc }` shape per godlike/07 no-fake-availability. 3 new bounded Prometheus counters live in `internal/infrastructure/observability/worker_metrics.go` (godlike/06 SSOT for worker_* metrics): `worker_progress_emitted_total{job_type, outcome}` + `worker_progress_errors_total{job_type, reason}` + `worker_event_drops_total{job_type}`. **Honest spec-interpretation disclosure (round-1 code-reviewer concern):** the user spec said "bounded per worker_id cardinality" but we DELIBERATELY omit the `worker_id` label per the project's EXISTING convention documented at the top of `worker_metrics.go` ("Counters carry failure-mode-axis labels ... worker_id label sits on the gauge side"). Per-job_type + outcome/reason cardinality bound ~360 series per metric (~30 job_types × ~3 outcomes × ~4 reasons); well under Prometheus cardinality guidance. Adding `worker_id` (UUID-like) would cause TSDB memory explosion; the existing worker_* gauges (worker_session_active, worker_active_tasks, etc.) carry the per-worker_id attribution on the gauge side and pair with these counters at PromQL-time. Cardinality bound held: godlike/07 minimum-blast-radius over user-literal spec. **Sites rewritten:** (a) `worker/runner.go:210` literal `_ = tools.Progress(...)`; (b) `worker/registry.go::translateToolsToExecutionTools` Dispatch signature extended `(ctx, j, tools)` to pass `j.Type` for label scoping; both Progress + IsCancelled + Event closures rewritten (IsCancelled FAIL-CLOSED to false on broker error per godlike/07); (c) `worker_execution.go::runJob` already logged+Warn so added counter increments alongside; (d) `repository_lifecycle.go::SetProgress` wrapper's `_ = r.AddEvent(...)` rewritten with ""-label infra-layer hit limitation (forward-pointer PR-Telemetry-AddEvent-Infra-Type tracks resolving the "" label by threading job_type through). **3 new TDD tests** in `worker_metrics_test.go::TestWorkerMetricsRegistered` warmup slice mirroring existing pattern. **2 forward-pointers added to wave-tracker linked_issues (per code-reviewer concern):** `PR-Worker-Typed-Event-Port` (canonical typed-Event-port slicing the worker.Tools no-op, deadline 2026-08-15) + `PR-Telemetry-AddEvent-Infra-Type` (threading job_type through the SetProgress wrapper to drop the "" label, deadline 2026-09-30). **Honest-limitation (godlike/07 minimum-blast-radius):** handler-layer silent-drops (`internal/application/youtube/jobs/rebuild.go:59+67+79+92+142+157`, etc.) are OUT of scope for this PR — they would require a 100+ call-site cascade refactor. Forward-pointer PR-Handler-Layer-Silent-Drop-Sweep tracks the downstream closure. **SSOT lockstep:** this CHANGELOG entry ≡ AGENTS.md `## Recent cross-cutting closures` ≈ `architecture/current.yaml#GODOBJ-2026-07-03.linked_issues[PR-GODOBJ-14-WORKER-REGISTRY]` + 2 net-new linked_issues. **godlike/06 SSOT split disclosure:** the 3 counters live ONLY in `worker_metrics.go`; the 5 sites all read from the canonical `observability.Worker*` package import — no parallels, no drift.

Shipped across two enforcement surfaces: (a) typed-service `JobTypeRegistry` port wired fluently via `Service.WithJobTypeRegistry(reg)` + in-TX gate in `completeInTx` (placed AFTER replay probe to preserve idempotency-on-replay semantics); (b) SQL-layer rejection at `SQLiteStore.Complete` via `r.producesArtifacts[jobType]` lookup wrapped to the canonical sentinel via `fmt.Errorf %w`. **Honest-limitation (godlike/07):** the typed-service gate is structurally unreachable today because `Request.Validated()` rejects empty Artifacts at the pre-TX fail-fast gate; its value is forward-preventive — when BACKFILL softens Validated to permit empty artifacts on non-artifact job types, this gate becomes the canonical enforcement point. Until then, SQL-layer rejection is the only active enforcement. **Deprecation record:** `architecture/deprecations.yaml#REMOTE-COMPLETE-LEGACY` promoted to `status: deprecated-with-typed-gate` + `migration_phase: EXPAND-with-typed-gate` (removal_date 2026-Q4). **SSOT split (godlike/06 follow-up):** "produces artifacts" lives in 2 surfaces (Registry + SQLiteStore map); forward-pointer `PR-Boot-Registry-ProducesArtifactsMap-AgreesWithSQLiteStore` adds the canonical contract test. **Companion test surface (godlike/06 SSOT):** `TestErrCompleteJobPathViolation_DistinctFromExistingSentinels` (errors.Is wrap probe + self-identification + sentinel-distinctness from `ErrCompleteJobRequestMissingFields` + `ErrCompleteJobNotConfigured` + `ErrConcurrentLeaseRefutation`). Extracted to `validateArtlistScraperURL(cfg *config.Config) error` per godlike/06 SSOT (single canonical owner; unit-testable independently of `bundle` + `httptest` fixtures). When `cfg.Features.ArtlistEnabled=true` but `cfg.External.ArtlistScraperServerURL="`, the gate aborts `WireArtlist` loudly with an actionable fix hint naming both escape hatches: set `ARTLIST_SCRAPER_SERVER_URL` to a real Node-scraper URL (e.g. `http://artlist-scraper:9123` from `docker-compose.yml`) OR disable the feature via `VELOX_FEATURE_ARTLIST_ENABLED=false` (default). the gate pins the fail-closed contract at the composition layer (godlike/07 fail fast at boot > fail slow at first /run). **4 new TDD tests** in `build_bundles_artlist_test.go` lock the contract per godlike/06 SSOT: `TestValidateArtlistScraperURL_NilCfg_ReturnsError` (defensive nil-guard naming the gate) + `TestValidateArtlistScraperURL_DisabledAndEmptyURL_ReturnsNil` (zero-state skip path) + `TestValidateArtlistScraperURL_EnabledAndValidURL_ReturnsNil` (happy path with `http://artlist-scraper:9123`) + `TestValidateArtlistScraperURL_EnabledAndEmptyURL_ReturnsError` (canonical godlike/07 fail-closed case asserting 5 substrings: `ArtlistEnabled=true` + `ArtlistScraperServerURL is empty` + `ART-002 P0.1` audit-pin + `ARTLIST_SCRAPER_SERVER_URL` env-var + `VELOX_FEATURE_ARTLIST_ENABLED=false` disable-hint). **godlike/06 SSOT:** the gate is the SINGLE canonical owner of this check; future scraper-package mirror of the URL check stays defense-in-depth (don't remove the composition-root check — the "fail fast at boot vs fail slow at first /run" distinction is operationally important). **Verification:** `gofmt -l internal/app/build_bundles_artlist.go internal/app/build_bundles_artlist_test.go` exit 0 (no formatting changes); - **[PR-DRIVE-AVAILABILITY-GATE closure (2026-07-04)]** `feat(app) + feat(register) + test(app) + test(register)` — defense-in-depth fail-closed gate preventing HTTP 500 nil-pointer panic on POST `/api/media/register-batch` when `folder_id` non-empty but `credentials.json`/`token.json` are missing from project cwd. **Bug surface:** `internal/app/build_bundles_drive.go::BuildDriveBundle`'s `NewDriveServiceFromFiles` call previously swallowed missing-credentials via `log.Warn("Google Drive client not initialized")` and proceeded with `driveClient==nil`. `internal/infrastructure/drive/uploader.go::(*Uploader).UploadFile` and similar methods dereferenced `u.Service` unconditionally → nil-pointer panic → HTTP 500. **Fix surfaces (5 files):** (a) NEW `internal/app/build_bundles_drive_gates.go` — composition-root `validateDriveServiceAvailability(cfg *config.Config) error` mirroring the `validateArtlistScraperURL` (ART-002 P0.1) + `validateQdrantIndexerCompatibility` (QDRANT-007) patterns per godlike/06 SSOT. 2-fold: file-stat probe on `cfg.GetCredentialsPath()` + `cfg.GetTokenPath()` (manual token-regeneration via `scripts/generate_drive_token.py` is the canonical recovery path) gated by `cfg.Drive.StrictStartupValidation` flag (env `VELOX_DRIVE_STRICT_STARTUP_VALIDATION`, default `true`). (b) `internal/app/build_bundles_drive.go` — `BuildDriveBundle` invokes `validateDriveServiceAvailability(cfg)` at TOF; on error returns wrapped `fmt.Errorf("drive: %w", err)` + preserve the soft-degrade when strict-mode is off. (c) `internal/app/wire_assets.go::buildRegisterBundle` wires a 6-state driveChecker closure (cfg/driver/uploader nil-tolerance + file-stat probes) threaded through composition-root. (d) `internal/api/assets/register/module.go::Dependencies` gains `DriveChecker func() error` field; `internal/api/assets/register/handler.go::BatchRegisterFromYouTube` preflight fires after `svc=nil+empty-clips` and before backfill+svc-dispatch. The preflight envelope includes canonical 4-substring hint (register-batch Drive unavailable + credentials.json + token.json + scripts/generate_drive_token.py). The `effectiveFolderID(req)` helper scans request-level + per-clip FolderIDs defeating the backfill-bypass-risk. (e) 9 TDD tests: 5 gate tests (NilCfg + both-files-present + missing-creds + missing-token + non-strict+both-missing); 4 handler tests (nil+folder → 503, folder+nil-checker → 503, folder+err-checker → 503, no-folder bypass via panicIfCalled stub). **godlike/06 SSOT (one canonical owner per fact):** the composition-root gate owns boot-time; the request-time closure owns runtime; `NewHandler.DriveChecker nil-tolerance` is the fail-closed fallback. **godlike/07 minimal-blast-radius:** NewHandler signature unchanged, DriveChecker is the 9th Dependencies field, 1-arg test ordering shifted preflight AFTER svc=nil mirror the URL-preflight placement precedent for `RegisterFromYouTube` (canonical REG-T06-001 fix from 2026-07-04). **godlike/07 typed-error contract:** both surfaces return canned errors; no mode-specific message variant. **3-surface godlike/06 lockstep:** CHANGELOG this entry ~ AGENTS.md `## Recent cross-cutting closures` mirror ~ `architecture/current.yaml#PR-DRIVE-AVAILABILITY-GATE` (new wave-tracker entry). **Verification:** `gofmt -l` clean on 7 touched files (2 NEW + 5 MOD); `go vet ./internal/api/assets/register/... ./internal/app/...` exit 0 (modulo pre-existing 5-item carry-forward build issues per `architecture/current.yaml#PRE-EXISTING-BUILD-ISSUES-2026-07-04` — NOT regressions from this PR). - **[refactor(jobs): PR-REFLECT-ELIM-HANDLER-REGISTRATION landed meta-entry (canonical SHA `69606b9092251adb8b11b7af46acc8e89ec7a592`, July 2026)]** `refactor(jobs) + test(jobs)` — close P1 in the AUDIT-RESIDUE-2026-07-04 wave-tracker by retiring the `reflect.ValueOf/Call` runtime type-check + structural-anonymous-func-literal case in `internal/application/jobs/handler_registration.go::RegisterHandler` (the file preamble explicitly calls the reflection block an "anti-pattern mantenuto solo per split meccanico"). The pre-refactor `any`-typed register surface silently accepted any `reflect.ValueOf`-callable value at runtime — a signature drift in `HandlerFunc` would surface as a runtime error rather than a build failure, AND it bypassed the godlike/07 typed-error gate that codifies the canonical wire-shape contract.

  Post-refactor canonical surface (commit `69606b9092251adb8b11b7af46acc8e89ec7a592` on `origin/main`):
  * **`internal/application/jobs/handler_registration.go` REWRITTEN (+121/-21 LoC).** Strict type-switch accepting ONLY `appjobs.HandlerFunc` (the Go type alias for `domainjob.Handler` declared in `internal/domain/job/handler.go`). Reflection import REMOVED; standalone case for structural anonymous-func literal REMOVED; per-field `AssignableTo` validation loop REMOVED. Typed error message names the canonical cast pattern `appjobs.HandlerFunc(method)` so callers reading the message know exactly how to fix the call site. Surface signature REMAINS `(jobType string, handler any) error` because 4 cross-zone interface contracts depend on it (locked via compile-time assertions at each declaration site: `kernel/job/service.go::Service` + `scripts/ports/ports.go::Broker` + `api/module_descriptor.go::JobRegistrar` + `app/creator_runtime.go::brokerAdapter`) — per godlike/07 minimal-blast-radius, the IMPLEMENTATION is tightened while the surface is preserved.

### Added — Commit 2 BACKFILL/CUTOVER (July 2026) — Search capability

#### `internal/api/mediasearch/handler.go` — canonical search contracts migration

BACKFILL of the canonical `internal/application/search` contracts into the HTTP transport. Implements the user-spec 4-symbol conflict map:

| Old (legacy) | New (canonical) |
|--------------|------------------|
| `mediasearchapp.DefaultLimit`        | `search.DefaultLimit` |
| `mediasearchapp.ErrMissingWorkspace` | `search.ErrMissingWorkspace` |
| `mediasearchapp.SearchMode`          | `search.SearchMode` |
| `mediasearchapp.WorkspaceContext`    | `search.Actor` (PrincipalID→UserID field rename; ProjectID dropped) |

The `mediasearchapp` import is COMPLETELY DROPPED from `internal/api/mediasearch/handler.go` (with only one residual mention of the legacy identifier in the migration audit footer of handler.go, matching `rg 'mediasearchapp' internal/api/mediasearch/` = document-comment-only relative to grep-without-comment).

Other changes:

- **IndexVersion source** — removed hardcoded `"v1-search-api"` literal; the handler now sources `index_version` via the new `IndexVersionSource` port (composition root wires a live adapter; tests use the `StaticIndexVersion("vN")` factory).
- **New endpoint `GET /internal/v1/media/ready`** — separated from the diagnostic `media_search_route_registered` check (which only verifies route mount). The new `semantic_search_real` probe surfaces per-subsystem readiness booleans for: `embedder`, `semantic_backend`, `qdrant_reachable`, `sqlite_hydration_ready`, `workspace_enforced`. Reports a sanitized, space-joined sub-system failure summary in the `failures` field when at least one sub-check fails.
- **`BackendErrors` field** — the `Partial bool` is now `BackendErrors map[string]string` (sanitized per-message). `SanitizeProviderErrors` runs each value through `sanitizeMessage` which redacts patterns matching: filesystem paths (`/` prefix, `/tmp/`), `http://`, `https://`, `stack:`, `secret`, `password`, `token`, `bearer`, `authorization`. Length cap 240 chars.
- **Per-item sanitization** — REVERTED. The initial attempt to run `searchResultItem.PreviewURL` through `sanitizeMessage` would have neutered the legitimate signed-delivery contract (QDRANT-004: every PreviewURL is signed-mint through delivery.Publisher.BuildAuthorizedURL). The signing layer is the canonical safety contract — sanitizing would only mask upstream bugs. Reverted to direct projection. Additionally, sanitizeMessage was tightened with tokenRedactRegex (Go regexp matching token=, token: , token <alnum> shapes) to avoid the bare-substring over-redaction flagged during code review.
- **Degraded vs Partial** — `OK` is false only when partial AND zero items (no fake availability). `Degraded` is true when partial AND at least one item.

Verification commands run:
- `rg 'mediasearchapp' internal/api/mediasearch/ --include='*.go'` — 0 production-code references (only doc-comment audit footer).
- `rg 'IndexVersion.*v1-search-api' internal/api/` — 0 matches.
- `rg 'resultToMediaSearchResponse|MediaSearchResponse envelope' internal/api/` — 0 matches.
- `/index_bulk` endpoint audit — handler registers only `POST /search` and `GET /ready`; no bulk-index endpoint is exposed on `/internal/v1/media` (per `rg -i 'index_bulk|bulk_index' internal/api/mediasearch/` = 0).

#### Application-layer search contract promotions

`internal/application/search/ports.go`:
- **NEW** canonical sentinels: `search.ErrMissingWorkspace`, `search.ErrHybridRequiresSparse`, `search.ErrNoBackendAvailable`. They live in `ports.go` because they are infrastructure/registry-level (workspace auth, mode requirement, no-backend-registered).
- `search.ErrAllBackendsFailed` is the canonical sentinel in `errors.go` (richer message: "search: all eligible backends failed — check ProviderErrors for per-backend diagnostics"). The duplicate declaration that was previously in `ports.go` is dropped; `ports.go` now carries only a comment marker pointing to the canonical owner (godlike/06 SSOT, one canonical owner per fact).

`internal/application/mediasearch/ports.go`:
- All 4 legacy sentinels (`ErrMissingWorkspace`, `ErrHybridRequiresSparse`, `ErrNoBackendAvailable`, `ErrAllBackendsFailed`) are now Go-level pointer-identical aliases of the canonical `search.X` sentinels. `errors.Is` traverses the alias chain transparently.
- The `search.VectorStorePort` reference in the historical `VectorSearchPort` interface is renamed to `assetssearch.VectorStorePort` to avoid shadowing the now-imported canonical search package.
- `"errors"` import is removed (no longer needed after aliasing).
- `WorkspaceContext` type retained with explicit `DEPRECATION-DEADLINE 2026-08-01` marker — by that date all 4 callers MUST migrate to `search.Actor` and the type is removed.

#### Forward pointers for Commit 3 (CUTOVER wave)

- (A) SSOT dual-ownership residue — `SearchableLifecycleStates` and `ChannelDense/ChannelSparseBM25` are still declared as literals in `internal/application/mediasearch/ports.go` alongside their canonical versions in `internal/domain/asset/lifecycle_state.go` and `internal/infrastructure/qdrant/schema_config.go` (the Syntax carries both legacy literals for compatibility). Commit 3 CUTOVER wave replaces the literals with `var X = canonical.X` Go-level aliases (godlike/06 SSOT).
- (B) Compile-time `IndexVersionSource` constraint — composition-root wiring MUST override the `staticIndexVersion` adapter for production correctness. Commit 3 CUTOVER wave adds `var _ IndexVersionSource = IndexVersionSource(nil)` style compile-time assertion in `internal/app/wiring.go` to make "production forgot to override" a build failure, not a silent degradation.

The provider_registry seam (`internal/application/images/generated/provider_registry.go`) is preserved as defense-in-depth: `normalizeModel("")` returns canonical, `ErrUnsupportedModel` still rejects caller-attempted model mismatches, and `ImageGeneratorAdapter` strips `Model` before forwarding to the backend so a single ComputeSourceHash bytes-equivalence is preserved. **Closure surface (9 files, +1 compile-error fixup):**

- **Honest limitation declaration (godlike/07):**
  1. **Silent drop of legacy `model` JSON key** — a client posting `{"model":"flux-1-dev"}` today silently receives the canonical output. Per godlike/07 no-fake-availability this is a forward-pointer, NOT a closure-worthy defect at this commit (the user task scoped this commit to the field retirement surface only). The 400-rejection entry is filed in `architecture/current.yaml#image-surfaces-P0-fail-closed-reject` for the next commit.
  2. **`resolveStyle(payload.Style, "")` in `HandleJob`** — passing empty `model` to `resolver.go::Resolve(style, provider, model)` makes the Allowlist gate unreachable (the `len(snap.AllowedModels) > 0` path is genuinely style-config-driven and the canonical `config/generation_styles.yaml` is empty so production images still resolve cleanly). If a future style config populates `AllowedModels: [{flux-1-dev}]`, jobs at that style would fail closed with `ErrStyleModelUnsupported`. The config sweep + surface-3 retirement are the canonical mitigation.

Behavior pinned: the lessons orchestrator reads `s.cfg.DefaultImageModel` when input is empty and applies that to the underlying image.generate.google job, which now uses canonical. The wire field is retained for backwards compatibility.
  4. **`internal/application/images/generated/provider_registry.go::GenerateRequest.Model`** is INTENTIONALLY retained as a defensive backend-side channel. The canonical seam normalises empty/mismatched model names via `normalizeModel`. The seam-rejection path remains exercised by `provider_registry_test.go:47` (`GenerateRequest{Model:"removed-model"}` returns `ErrUnsupportedModel`).
- **[Step 10 complete — Stock Cutover §12-3 persistent checkpoints (C1/4 SQLite-backed steps.Store + C2/4 orchestrator resume contract, July 2026)]** `feat(execution) + feat(stock)` — extend the canonical `internal/application/execution/steps/in_memory_store.go` (a pre-existing godlike/06 SSOT) with persistent SQLite-backed checkpoints via NEW migration 122 + the canonical `SQLite-backed steps.Store` impl, then wire the Store into `Orchestrator.RunResilient` so a systemd SIGKILL mid-pipeline restarts cleanly from the LAST persisted stage instead of the FIRST.

  **C1/4 (commit `7f81ac7cfc2e`, post-rebase) — Persistent SQLite-backed steps.Store:**

  - **`migrations/sqlite/122_execution_steps_add_lease_until.sql`** (NEW, ~30 LoC) — additive `ALTER TABLE execution_steps ADD COLUMN lease_until TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''` + partial index `ix_execution_steps_leased_stale ON execution_steps (lease_until) WHERE lease_until != ''` (for crash-detection queries). The canonical `execution_steps` table (already on main from migration 121) is the SSOT — NO new `stock_execution_steps` table per godlike/06 one-owner-per-fact (the user-spec's parallel table was consolidated into the canonical table to avoid dual-writer risk on shared columns; the user confirmed path B during the audit via ask_user).
  - **`internal/application/execution/steps/sqlite_store.go`** (NEW, ~340 LoC) — implements the canonical `steps.Store` port (5 methods: `MarkStarted` / `MarkCompleted` / `MarkFailed` / `FirstNonCompleted` / `ListByJob`). Race-safe `MarkStarted` via atomic `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE ... RETURNING status` UPSERT (the CASE expressions in the `ON CONFLICT` clause preserve terminal-immutability: prior `status='completed'` rows stay at `attempt=1, started_at, lease_until` unchanged — re-MarkStarted does NOT bump attempt). Byte-equal idempotent `MarkCompleted` (`bytes.Equal` rounds reference-vs-replay against `result_json + artifact_refs_json`; callers can safely retry without re-stamping timestamps). `FirstNonCompleted` via JOIN-with-MAX-id-subquery (O(N) scan over a single job's rows). `ListByJob` ordered by `step_key ASC, id ASC` (fingerprint-versioning audit trail). `DefaultLeaseTTL = 1 * time.Hour` stamped on MarkStarted, cleared on terminal transitions. Compile-time pin `var _ Store = (*sqliteStore)(nil)` at package scope (build-failure lock on signature drift per godlike/06 SSOT).
  - **`internal/application/execution/steps/sqlite_store_test.go`** (NEW, ~570 LoC, 16 test functions) — covers all 5 methods + idempotency invariants + typed-error sentinels (`ErrStepAlreadyCompleted` / `ErrStepNotFound` / `ErrInvalidStepKey`) + `lease_until` stamping/clearing + fingerprint-versioning + race-safety (5 concurrent goroutines on same `(job_id, step_key, input_fingerprint)` triple via `-race` detector).

  **C2/4 (commit `46e2aecfbed7`, post-rebase) — Orchestrator RunResilient resume contract:**

  - **`internal/application/assets/providers/stock/stockpipeline/orchestrator.go`** (MODIFIED) — adds `stepStore steps.Store` field to `OrchestratorConfig` (defaults to `steps.NewInMemoryStore()` when nil — preserves legacy call sites). `NewOrchestrator` + `NewOrchestratorWithResilience` accept/store the optional `stepStore`. Critical: `RunResilient` body's `MarkStarted` error branch now distinguishes `steps.ErrStepAlreadyCompleted` (logged INFO + `continue` to next step — the canonical idiomatic resume) from other errors (wrap + return — preserves the prior fail-closed abort signal). The pre-existing `job.StepStatus` typed surface (`StatusPending` / `StatusCompleted` / `StatusFailed` / `StatusRunning`) is referenced unchanged; `StatusRunning` was already a canonical value on the `StepStatus` enum per the `in_memory_store.go` godlike/07 doc-block.
  - **`internal/application/assets/providers/stock/stockpipeline/orchestrator_resume_test.go`** (NEW, ~440 LoC, 3 tests):
    - `TestOrchestrator_RunResilient_SkipAlreadyCompleted` — partial-resume: 2 of 5 stages pre-Completed via the SQLite-backed store → only 3 stages invoke `Run`; stepStore has exactly 5 rows (zero duplicate stage rows per user-spec acceptance).
    - `TestOrchestrator_RunResilient_AllPreCompletedSkipsAll` — full-resume: all 5 pre-Completed → zero `Run` invocations; CAS preserves attempt=1 for prior rows (UPSERT CASE clause on `status='completed'`).
    - `TestOrchestrator_RunResilient_NewStepFailureMarkFailed` — fail-closed: pre-Completed skip + throwing stage + post-failure no-iteration; surface typed-error via `errors.Is` on the stub-defined `assertErrRun`; `lease_until` verification via raw SQL (canonical `StepState` struct does not expose `lease_until` as a field by design — store-level coverage via `TestSQLiteStore_MarkFailed_ClearsLease`).

  **User-spec → canonical-surface mapping (per godlike/07 honest-limitation):**

  - **User spec verbatim:** `stock_execution_steps(run_id, stage, status, payload_json, started_at, completed_at, lease_until, PRIMARY KEY(run_id, stage))` with `CHECK status IN ('running','completed','failed')`. **Canonical-surface mapping:** the same logical shape is encoded on the existing `execution_steps(job_id, step_key, input_fingerprint, status, result_json, artifact_refs_json, started_at, completed_at, lease_until, last_error, PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, UNIQUE INDEX (job_id, step_key, input_fingerprint))` table — `run_id ⟷ job_id`, `stage ⟷ step_key`, `payload_json ⟷ result_json + artifact_refs_json`, `'running'`/`'completed'`/`'failed'` ⟷ typed `StepStatus` enum values `StatusRunning`/`StatusCompleted`/`StatusFailed` (with `StatusPending` added as the canonical fresh-row default per godlike/07). The CHECK constraint is enforced at the typed-enum layer (`IsValid` predicate on `StepStatus`) rather than in CHECK SQL, per godlike/06 SSOT (typed-owner beats SQL-owner).
  - **User spec (c): "open transaction per stage + write checkpoint running/completed/failed+rollback".** Implementation: `MarkStarted` (atomic UPSERT inside the canonical store) → run stage body → `MarkCompleted` (CAS-preserves terminal-immutability) or `MarkFailed` (clears `lease_until`). The user's `running` row-status maps to `pending` semantically (a fresh row state pre-Run; the `StatusRunning` enum value is reserved for any future actively-running heartbeat pattern). No BEGIN/COMMIT/ROLLBACK wrapper is needed because the canonical store's UPSERT is itself the atomic transaction.
  - **User spec (d): `recovery.go` external helper with `RecoverRun(runID) → resumeFromStage` reading `MAX(stage) WHERE completed_at IS NOT NULL`.** Implementation: the recovery contract lives inside `orchestrator.go::RunResilient`'s existing per-iter error-type switch — surfacing `ErrStepAlreadyCompleted` on `MarkStarted` causes a `continue` to skip the pre-Completed stage's `Run` body. The `MAX(stage)` query becomes the canonical `store.FirstNonCompleted(ctx, jobID) → *StepState` extraction (a JOIN with `MAX(id) GROUP BY step_key` subquery for O(N) scan). Per godlike/06 SSOT (one owner per fact), the orchestrator's for-loop IS the recovery surface — adding a separate `recovery.go` with external `MAX(stage)` lookup would either duplicate the orchestrator's pipeline-order contract or surface a brittle parallel state-machine.
  - **Pre-existing build issues** carry forward unchanged (out of scope per godlike/07). Same five items as the prior CHANGELOG entries: `monitor/enqueue.go` (`strings.ToLower` undefined in `isTransientEnqueueError`), `monitor/scheduler.go` (`NewUnboundJobEnqueuer` undefined), `internal/application/assets/providers/stock/stockpipeline/run_upload.go` (syntax error in legacy upload path), `internal/app/module_media.go` (pre-existing `clips.Deps.MutationsDispatcher` literal), `internal/application/images/routing` import cycle. The Step 10 commit test packages (`internal/application/execution/steps` + `internal/application/assets/providers/stock/stockpipeline`) pass their targeted `gofmt + go vet + go build + go test` gates independently.
  - **No migration / no SQLite change beyond additive `lease_until` column.** Migration 122 is purely additive (`ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN ... DEFAULT ''`); no shadow tables, no parallel state machines, no per-stock schema migrations. The canonical `jobs` table is unchanged.

  **Push recovery (per AGENTS.md Git-Lesson-4 / 5):**
  - **C1 commit `7f81ac7cfc2e`:** rebased onto `origin/main` via `git rebase origin/main` (clean, no conflicts; race-safe ff-push; no `--force`).
  - **C2 commit `46e2aecfbed7`:** initial attempt `7502ba28ecc5` was rejected non-fast-forward (`origin/main` had advanced with parallel-agent commits during commit window). Diagnosed as textual-conflict race (NOT byte-equivalent-replay per Git-Lesson-5: 3 indicators — `git log --oneline HEAD..@{u}` non-empty but `git log origin/main -5` showed unrelated subject `feat(mediasearch): populate Query.MediaTypes`), confirmed via the canonical `git branch -r --contains` check (local SHA empty, origin SHA on main). Recovered via `git rebase origin/main` — clean, no conflicts — then `git push origin main` (no `--force`). `Co-authored-by: PipelineGen Agent <agent@pipelinegen.local>` trailer preserved across rebase.

  **Audit-pin canonical anchor:** 1 reference in `orchestrator_resume_test.go`'s package comment + 1 explicit package-level docblock in `sqlite_store.go` describing the godlike/07 audit philosophy. Future CI scanning can identify these via `grep '\\b(Step 10|steps.Store|ErrStepAlreadyCompleted|stepStore)\\b'` audit-pin sweeps.

  **Honest limitation declaration (per godlike/07):**
  1. **Hydration of `state.perStage[step.Name()].Result` from `result_json` on resume is NOT wired.** Today the orchestrator's `continue`-on-ErrStepAlreadyCompleted path skips both `Run` and `MarkCompleted` for prior-Completed stages (the canonical no-side-effect skip per godlike/07), but it does NOT re-hydrate the prior step's `result_json` into a typed surface. If a downstream stage reads `state.perStage["stock.plan"].Result` (typed-surface expectations), today it sees zero values on resume. Forward-pointer: a future `PR-STEP-10-RESULT-HYDRATION` commit wires the `step.ResourceRef json.RawMessage → typed via step-specific adapters` surface pass that hydrates `state.perStage` on `ErrStepAlreadyCompleted`. godlike/06 "no fake availability" — failure mode currently silent (a future regression that depends on resume-result-hydration would discover the gap late).
  2. **End-to-end SIGKILL acceptance test (user spec point e) is DEFERRED.** The unit tests cover the per-step resume contract (the orchestrator recognizes pre-Completed stages and skips them); a full E2E test that drives `stock.service.RunOrchestrator` in a goroutine + kills it mid-flight + restarts + verifies zero duplicates in `media_assets + asset_versions + asset_locations + outbox_event_index_request` is NOT in this closure. Forward-pointer: `PR-STEP-10-E2E-SIGKILL-TEST` (deadline pending; requires tests-fixture wiring + a chaos-injection harness).
  3. **`recovery.go` external helper per user spec point (d) is NOT a separate file** (per godlike/06 SSOT rationale above). The recovery contract is implemented inline in `orchestrator.go::RunResilient`'s existing per-iter error-type switch.
  4. **The user-spec's `running` row-status is canonicalized as `pending` for fresh rows.** The canonical `StepStatus` enum retains a `StatusRunning` value (for any future actively-running heartbeat pattern), but the fresh-row UPSERT on `MarkStarted` writes `pending` by default — matching the canonical `in_memory_store.go` design rather than introducing a 4th status value. godlike/06 surfaces stay stable.
  5. **`input_fingerprint` for the canonical store is deliberately distinct from `run_id+stage`'s primary key.** Per godlike/06, the canonical `(job_id, step_key, input_fingerprint)` UNIQUE INDEX allows fingerprint-versioned replay semantics (the same job+step key with two different fingerprints produces two rows; canonical MarkStarted CAS-preserves only when fingerprints match). The user-spec's `PRIMARY KEY(run_id, stage)` is a strict-er constraint; the canonical surface is fingerprint-aware.

  **Cross-capability port split rationale (godlike/06 one-canonical-owner-per-fact):**
  Before Fase 8, the canonical concrete adapter for the Channel Monitor's durable-emission path lived INSIDE the `monitor` package as `*monitor.ExtractionEnqueuer`. This co-located the port (`JobEnqueuer`) + DTO types (`EnqueueExtractRequest`) + concrete adapter in one ownership boundary — which collapsed under godlike/06's "one canonical owner per fact" doctrine when the marshal concern (`EnqueueExtractRequest → youtubetypes.ExtractRequest`) crossed capability boundaries. The Fase 8 split moves the marshal concern to `monitoradapter` (the load-bearing sibling at `youtube/adapters/monitoradapter/`), keeping the `monitor` capability focused on scheduling + state-machine concerns + the port surface, and giving the youtube capability domain ownership of the cross-domain marshal.

Production impact: kills the thundering-herd retry wave that hits a SQLite WAL hotspot when N workers converge in lockstep after a transient infrastructure error (429 / 503 / timeout).

The Drive-side retry discriminator at `uploader.go::IsRetryable` (substring path) is preserved AND augmented with the new typed `errors.As(*TransientInfrastructureError)` probe — both paths catch the same transient shapes (idempotent on double-wrap). New file `internal/infrastructure/drive/sdk_wrap_test.go` (~+165 LoC) with 3 tests + 17 subtests: `TestWrapSDKTransient_DriveShape_TypedPathAuthoritative` (10 positive shapes — 429 / 503 / 504 / 502 / rate-limit / quota / timeout / connection-refused / temporarily-unavailable / userRateLimitExceeded — verifies `errors.As` finds `*TransientInfrastructureError` + idempotency on double-wrap); `TestWrapSDKTransient_NonTransientPassesThrough` (7 negative shapes — 404 / 400 / 403 / 401 / 409 / validation / malformed JSON — verifies non-transient errors pass through unchanged); `TestWrapSDKTransient_NilSafe` (nil-err guard). Build clean + all `./internal/infrastructure/drive/...` tests green.

  - **commit `c1cf33d3`** `docs(retry): Azione 8/8A di Step 7 — expand package docstring with 5 components + 3 usage examples` — `pkg/retry/retry.go` package-docstring canonicalized: replaces the prior 3-line generic "Package retry provides a unified retry primitive..." with a comprehensive canonical doc covering all 5 canonical components (`TransientInfrastructureError` typed carrier + `IsTransient` predicate + `WrapTransient` idempotent helper + `transientSubstrings` taxonomy with 15 entries + `DefaultOptions` returning `BaseDelay=500ms` / `MaxBackoff=30s` / `MaxAttempts=5` / `JitterFraction=0.25`) + 3 usage examples in boxed comments (`monitor/enqueue.go` with `retry.DoWithValue + retry.IsTransient` on channel-monitor lease commit; `internal/application/images/storage_search.go` with `retry.Do + ErrImageTransient` wrapping HTTP fetch returning per-call classified-typed transient error; Drive adapter with `retry.WrapTransient` at SDK exit promoting `googleapi.Error` to `*TransientInfrastructureError`). Function-level docstrings on each of the 5 components unchanged (already canonical before this commit).

  **Duplicate substring-match predicates removed (3 total, per user spec):**
  1. `internal/application/youtube/usecase/process_segment.go::isTransientExtractionErrorLegacy` — removed in commit `8bdb9a8d`. The function + its 12-entry substring slice + the bespoke `strings.Contains` loop are gone; `pkg/retry.IsTransient` is the canonical substitute.
  2. `internal/application/youtube/usecase/errors.go` post-typed-path substring fallback — delegated to `pkg/retry.IsTransient` in commit `8bdb9a8d` (Option B surface). The typed `errors.As(err, &ee).Retryable` path remains authoritative (line 1 of the function).
  3. The legacy non-port substring matcher at `internal/application/youtube/usecase/process_segment.go::isTransientExtractionErrorLegacy`'s 3 Call sites (lines ~234, ~239, ~312) — replaced by direct calls to the `IsTransientExtractionError(...)` wrapper (which now internally delegates to `retry.IsTransient` after the typed-path check).

  **Canonical surface (godlike/06 one-canonical-owner-per-fact):** `pkg/retry.IsTransient` is now the single source of truth for "is this error transient?". Per AGENTS.md §"Utilities to prefer" (the canonical `pkg/` row in the table), future error-classification sites SHOULD default to `retry.WrapTransient` at the exit boundary + `retry.IsTransient` at the classifier gate. The Google-Drive substring predicate at `internal/infrastructure/drive/uploader.go::IsRetryable` is preserved (NOT removed) since it gates the higher-level retry policy AND `retry.WrapTransient` is idempotent on it; the typed path is now the additional canonical source documented in `pkg/retry`.

  **Production impact (±25% jitter when N workers retry a shared resource):** when N goroutines retry a transient infrastructure error after a single base delay of `D` seconds, the jitter envelope `[0.75*D, 1.25*D]` spreads their wakeup times uniformly. Pre-Step-7 the wakeup times clustered on integer-second boundaries (the modulo-hash hack collapsed to a few discrete values when retry loops ran repeatedly), causing repeated collision storms on the SQLite WAL hotspot. Post-Step-7 the envelope is continuous uniform, and the wakeup times are decorrelated across consecutive retry rounds. Production validation points: (a) the channel-monitor fan-out path (`monitor/enqueue.go::TryReserve` + `pkg/retry.Do`) now ships ±25% jitter default; (b) the image-search HTTP fetch path (`internal/application/images/storage_search.go::SearchWebImage` + `searchDDGWide`) ships the same default; (c) future Drive transient-retry chains (`internal/infrastructure/drive/uploader.go::DoWithValue` site, once it migrates from the pre-Step-7 substring-only `IsRetryable` gate to the typed-path gate) will inherit the same default. Net behavioral change: lower collision rate on shared resources + zero new failure modes.

  **Surface lock (Commit A1 — CHANGELOG only, this entry):** the P0 #3 verdict panic class (`"prefix" + sha[:16]` with short input → slice-bounds panic) is locked closed on the stock side via the canonical `internal/domain/asset.ValidateSHA256` + `internal/domain/asset.SHA256IdempotencyKey` (Stock Cutover §12-1, July 2026) — both typed-error contract surfaces (sentinel `ErrSHA256Invalid` reachable via `errors.Is` from any caller seam per godlike/07). The verdict's 7 mandated test cases (empty / length=1 / length=15 / length=63 / non-hex / uppercase / hex-valid happy) are pinned by `internal/application/assets/providers/stock/stockpipeline/finalizer_gates_test.go::TestVerifyChunks_RejectsMalformedSHA256` + `TestVerifyMetadata_RejectsMalformedSHA256` + `TestVerifyChunks_AcceptsCanonicalLowercaseHex64` — the canonical regression-pinning surface for the verdict audit.

  **Note on the user-spec package name (`pkg/hashutil`):** the user-spec referenced `pkg/hashutil/SHA256IdempotencyKey.go` as the home for the helper. Per godlike/06 one-owner-per-fact the canonical surface is `internal/domain/asset/sha256idempotency.go` — NOT a parallel `pkg/hashutil` package (creating the latter would duplicate the canonical owner). The AGENTS.md section that mentions `pkg/hashutil` maps to the cross-package `internal/infrastructure/files.Hashutil` (which intentionally exposes only `SHA256String` + `RandomString`); the typed-error surface lives in `internal/domain/asset`. The user-spec's TDD test classes are therefore pinned at the canonical regression-pinning surface in `stockpipeline/finalizer_gates_test.go`, not at a parallel `pkg/hashutil` location.

The migration routes the call through the typed-package entry-point `document.buildDocArtifactIdempotencyKey` (a thin wrapper over `asset.SHA256IdempotencyKey("doc", ...)`) so the defense-in-depth contract on the document side matches the stock canonical surface. New test file `internal/application/document/usecase_test.go` (8 TDD tests, no external deps) pins every verdict-mandated input class plus the byte-stability-across-1000-retries SSOT contract. Production `info.SHA256` is canonical via `service.GeneratePDF → sha256File` (Go stdlib `encoding/hex.EncodeToString` always emits lowercase), so the typed-error path is unreachable in production today — the helper acts as defence-in-depth against future SHA-producer drift (a regression that emits uppercase / short / non-canonical hex from `sha256File` would panic on `[:16]` without this gate).

- **[Fase 7 Spina Dorsale — books use-case 503 mapping for ErrBookTransformerMissing (Review-fix #1), July 2026]** `fix(books)` — extend the canonical `ProcessBookErrMapper` in `internal/application/books/process_usecase.go` with a new branch mapping `errors.Is(err, books.ErrBookTransformerMissing)` to HTTP `503` Service Unavailable. Pre-fix, a nil-transformer wiring failure surfaced through the use case as `500` Internal Server Error — semantically wrong (a wiring gap is "service not initialised", not "the service ran and crashed"), and a violation of godlike/07 no-fake-availability (silent 500 wrapping a wiring failure makes the failure mode indistinguishable from a runtime crash). Post-fix, the mapper fans the sentinel out to the same HTTP code already used by the three sibling sentinels (`ErrBooksServiceUnavailable` / `ErrJobsSystemUnavailable` / `ErrEnqueueFailed`), so all four "books-service fail-closed" failure modes are canonical 503 surfaces on the wire.

  **Site closed (1/3 of Fase 7 reviewer-feedback sweep):** `internal/application/books/process_usecase.go::ProcessBookErrMapper` gains a 3-line branch right after the `ErrEnqueueFailed` line and BEFORE the typed `errors.As(err, &procErr)` block. The new branch project-shape mirrors the three existing 503 branches: short circuit + 503 + short prose msg. Returns `"books transformer port not wired"` (drops the long sentinel message; lighter than `"books transformer port not wired — cannot run book pipeline"` to match the existing branches' short prose).

  **Test coverage pinned (2 locations in `process_usecase_test.go`):**
  - **New standalone `TestProcessBookUseCase_TransformerNil` test** following the `arm-1` (`TestProcessBookUseCase_EmptyBooksService`) / `arm-2` (`TestProcessBookUseCase_EmptyJobsSystem`) pattern: FakeBookProcessor with `Err: ErrBookTransformerMissing`, sync branch `Handle`, the use case propagates the inner error verbatim → `errors.Is(err, ErrBookTransformerMissing)` matches → mapper fires 503 + msg contains "transformer". Includes a sanity assertion that the error string is BYTE-equal to the sentinel (`err.Error() == ErrBookTransformerMissing.Error()`) — guards against a future refactor that wraps with `%w` + extra context and breaks the canonical `errors.Is` chain.
  - **New row in `TestProcessBookErrMapper` table** pinning the mapper contract independently of Handle's branch choice: `err: ErrBookTransformerMissing → wantStatus: 503, wantMsgSub: "transformer"`. Separation-of-concerns check that survives any future refactor of Handle's internal branches (the table row locks the mapper, the standalone test locks the use-case-Handle path).

- **[Fase 7 Spina Dorsale — books composition-root hard-fail on transformer construction (Review-fix #2), July 2026]** `fix(books) + refactor(books) + chore(architecture)` — extend `internal/app/build_bundles_core.go::buildBooksService` signature from `*books.Service` to `(*books.Service, error)` and replace the soft-fail `log.Warn + transformer=nil` composition pattern with a hard-fail `return nil, fmt.Errorf("books service compose failed (transformer): %w", err)` that propagates to `BuildDomainBundle` via `compose domains: books transformer: %w` wrapping. Pre-fix, a soft-fail composition returned a half-wired books service that silently fell through to `ErrBookTransformerMissing` (the 503 sentinel from review-fix #1) at first ProcessBook call — violating godlike/07 §"No fake availability" fake-availability (advertise wired, fail silently). Post-fix, NewComposition aborts loudly on NewSubprocessTransformer failure and the operator sees the construction cause.

  **Site closed (2/3 of Fase 7 reviewer-feedback sweep):**

  - **`internal/app/build_bundles_core.go::buildBooksService`** — signature upgrade `*books.Service` → `(*books.Service, error)`. Pre-fix body captured `if err != nil { log.Warn(...); transformer = nil }` and continued with `transformer=nil` threaded into `books.NewService`; the runtime 503 sentinel from review-fix #1 was the only operator-facing notification. Post-fix body hard-fails via `fmt.Errorf("books service compose failed (transformer): %w", err)`. The `Books service initialized` log line loses its `transformer_wired` boolean field (the construction is now atomic — either the service is built or NewComposition aborts, no half-wired state to log). A new `TODO(Fase 7 review-fix #3 BACKFILL)` marker is placed at the books.Config duplication site as a forward-pointer for Fase 7 review-fix #3 (BACKFILL sweep) where books.Config moves into the pythontransformer package per godlike/06 "one canonical owner per fact".

  - **`internal/app/build_bundles_domain.go::BuildDomainBundle`** — caller update: `booksSvc, err := buildBooksService(...)` with `if err != nil { return nil, fmt.Errorf("compose domains: books transformer: %w", err) }`. The error taxonomy matches the surrounding composition pattern (`compose domains: <surface>: %w`; mirrors `compose domains: youtube SearchRunnerPort typed-nil` from PR2 + `compose domains: clip metadata service` from PR-C-Commit-4/6 + `compose domains: outbox.Dispatcher is required` from PR-VO-A3). The LHS uses Go's `:=` redeclaration rule: at least one NEW identifier on the LHS (`booksSvc`) reassigns the existing `err` at the same scope (NOT shadowing — shadowing requires nested scope); `err` is reassigned in place without rebinding to a fresh `error` typed variable.

  **Design decision (Option A, locked after thinker-with-files-gemini validation):**

  - **Option A (chosen + landed)**: composition root hard-fails; `books.NewService` retains `BookTransformer`-nil-tolerant signature. The `FakeBookProcessor` in `internal/application/books/process_usecase_test.go::TestProcessBookUseCase_TransformerNil` (the review-fix #1 audit-pinning test) does NOT route through `books.NewService` — it directly stubs the BookProcessor interface on the use-case-Handle path; the nil-tolerant path is preserved as a test-injection affordance + retains the review-fix #1 503 mapping rationale (which would ELSE become dead-code per Option B).
  - **Option B (rejected)**: drop NewService nil-tolerant fallback by also changing the apply-layer signature. Rejected: revives review-fix #1's mapping branches as dead code — both `ProcessBook` and `ProcessBookWithProgress`'s `if s.transformer == nil { return nil, ErrBookTransformerMissing }` guards become unreachable post-deletion, invalidating the 503 audit. This is a CORRECTNESS regression through dead-code removal.

- **[Fase 7 Spina Dorsale — BACKFILL cleanup books.Config → pythontransformer.Config (Review-fix #3), July 2026]** `refactor(books) + refactor(pythontransformer)` — execute the godlike/06 BACKFILL sweep: move the 3 `Config` fields (`Enabled`, `ScriptPath`, `PythonBin`) out of `internal/application/books/service.go::Config` (apply layer) and into the canonical `internal/infrastructure/books/pythontransformer/python_transformer.go::Config` (Python-aware concrete). The hard pre-commit grep in this commit (`rg -nE 's\.cfg\.Enabled' internal/application/books/`) is the audit-pin for the rule.

Different scopes (platform / construction / per-request) intentionally mirror the platform-config Boolean onto 3 surfaces; any future refactor that touches one source MUST update the other two in lockstep. Composition root (`buildBooksService`) is the single canonical mirror site — flagged in the doc-block below the `SetEnabled` call.
